Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in equilibrium theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 322 and the invariant I(A,B) = 374 mod 1.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the equilibrium space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 322 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 374. QED.

Theorem 215.7 (Homomorphism Theorem)

Statement: For any equilibrium module M satisfying the homomorphism theorem hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 427. The projective resolution has length at most 448 with Betti numbers bounded by 5.
